{"parentUuid":"b86baf8a-e66e-4c72-80fe-55be82785354","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"dcde5cab-f969-40a6-a5c9-2d2030d94f16","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01HS1UvEJ3rVkcGAPdyf5xQt","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t/**\n2\t * Shareable Puzzle State URLs\n3\t *\n4\t * Encode any game state into a compact URL hash parameter for sharing.\n5\t *\n6\t * Format: #s=<gameId>.<version>.<base64url>\n7\t *\n8\t * Example: #s=water-sort.1.eJyrVkqtKEnNSwUA...\n9\t *\n10\t * Usage:\n11\t *   const hash = encodeState('water-sort', state);\n12\t *   window.location.hash = hash;\n13\t *\n14\t *   const { gameId, state } = decodeState(window.location.hash) ?? {};\n15\t */\n16\t\n17\timport { deflateRaw, inflateRaw } from 'pako';\n18\t\n19\t// ─── Constants ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n20\t\n21\tconst CURRENT_VERSION = 1;\n22\tconst HASH_PREFIX = 's=';\n23\tconst SEPARATOR = '.';\n24\tconst MAX_URL_LENGTH = 2000;\n25\t\n26\t// ─── Encoding ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n27\t\n28\t/**\n29\t * Convert a Uint8Array to URL-safe base64 (no padding).\n30\t * @param {Uint8Array} bytes\n31\t * @returns {string}\n32\t */\n33\tfunction toBase64Url(bytes) {\n34\t  // Build binary string in chunks to avoid stack overflow on large inputs\n35\t  const CHUNK = 8192;\n36\t  let binary = '';\n37\t  for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i += CHUNK) {\n38\t    binary += String.fromCharCode(...bytes.subarray(i, i + CHUNK));\n39\t  }\n40\t  return btoa(binary)\n41\t    .replace(/\\+/g, '-')\n42\t    .replace(/\\//g, '_')\n43\t    .replace(/=+$/, '');\n44\t}\n45\t\n46\t/**\n47\t * Convert URL-safe base64 back to Uint8Array.\n48\t * @param {string} b64url\n49\t * @returns {Uint8Array}\n50\t */\n51\tfunction fromBase64Url(b64url) {\n52\t  const b64 = b64url\n53\t    .replace(/-/g, '+')\n54\t    .replace(/_/g, '/');\n55\t  // Add padding back\n56\t  const padded = b64 + '='.repeat((4 - (b64.length % 4)) % 4);\n57\t  const binary = atob(padded);\n58\t  const bytes = new Uint8Array(binary.length);\n59\t  for (let i = 0; * Shareable Puzzle State URLs\n *\n * Encode any game state into a compact URL hash parameter for sharing.\n *\n * Format: #s=<gameId>.<version>.<base64url>\n *\n * Example: #s=water-sort.1.eJyrVkqtKEnNSwUA...\n *\n * Usage:\n *   const hash = encodeState('water-sort', state);\n *   window.location.hash = hash;\n *\n *   const { gameId, state } = decodeState(window.location.hash) ?? {};\n */\n\nimport { deflateRaw, inflateRaw } from 'pako';\n\n// ─── Constants ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nconst CURRENT_VERSION = 1;\nconst HASH_PREFIX = 's=';\nconst SEPARATOR = '.';\nconst MAX_URL_LENGTH = 2000;\n\n// ─── Encoding ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/**\n * Convert a Uint8Array to URL-safe base64 (no padding).\n * @param {Uint8Array} bytes\n * @returns {string}\n */\nfunction toBase64Url(bytes) {\n  // Build binary string in chunks to avoid stack overflow on large inputs\n  const CHUNK = 8192;\n  let binary = '';\n  for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i += CHUNK) {\n    binary += String.fromCharCode(...bytes.subarray(i, i + CHUNK));\n  }\n  return btoa(binary)\n    .replace(/\\+/g, '-')\n    .replace(/\\//g, '_')\n    .replace(/=+$/, '');\n}\n\n/**\n * Convert URL-safe base64 back to Uint8Array.\n * @param {string} b64url\n * @returns {Uint8Array}\n */\nfunction fromBase64Url(b64url) {\n  const b64 = b64url\n    .replace(/-/g, '+')\n    .replace(/_/g, '/');\n  // Add padding back\n  const padded = b64 + '='.repeat((4 - (b64.length % 4)) % 4);\n  const binary = atob(padded);\n  const bytes = new Uint8Array(binary.length);\n  for (let i = 0; i < binary.length; i++) {\n    bytes[i] = binary.charCodeAt(i);\n  }\n  return bytes;\n}\n\n/**\n * Encode a game state to a URL hash string.\n *\n * @param {string} gameId - Game identifier (e.g. 'water-sort')\n * @param {*} state - Serializable game state object\n * @param {number} [version=1] - Schema version for forward compatibility\n * @returns {string} URL hash string starting with '#s='\n */\nexport function encodeState(gameId, state, version = CURRENT_VERSION) {\n  if (!gameId || typeof gameId !== 'string') {\n    throw new Error('gameId must be a non-empty string');\n  }\n\n  const json = JSON.stringify(state);\n  const compressed = deflateRaw(json, { level: 6 });\n  const b64 = toBase64Url(compressed);\n  const hash = `#${HASH_PREFIX}${gameId}${SEPARATOR}${version}${SEPARATOR}${b64}`;\n\n  // If URL would be too long, fall back to compact level+moves encoding\n  if (hash.length > MAX_URL_LENGTH && state && state.levelId !== undefined) {\n    return encodeCompact(gameId, state, version);\n  }\n\n  return hash;\n}\n\n/**\n * Compact fallback encoding: only levelId + moves list.\n * Used when full state would exceed URL length limits.\n *\n * @param {string} gameId\n * @param {*} state\n * @param {number} version\n * @returns {string}\n */\nfunction encodeCompact(gameId, state, version) {\n  const compact = {\n    levelId: state.levelId,\n    moves: state.moves ?? [],\n    _compact: true,\n  };\n  const json = JSON.stringify(compact);\n  const compressed = deflateRaw(json, { level: 6 });\n  const b64 = toBase64Url(compressed);\n  return `#${HASH_PREFIX}${gameId}${SEPARATOR}${version}${SEPARATOR}${b64}`;\n}\n\n// ─── Decoding ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/**\n * Decode a URL hash string back to { gameId, version, state }.\n *\n * @param {string} hash - URL hash string (with or without leading '#')\n * @returns {{ gameId: string, version: number, state: * } | null}\n */\nexport function decodeState(hash) {\n  if (!hash || typeof hash !== 'string') return null;\n\n  // Strip leading '#'\n  const raw = hash.startsWith('#') ? hash.slice(1) : hash;\n\n  if (!raw.startsWith(HASH_PREFIX)) return null;\n\n  const payload = raw.slice(HASH_PREFIX.length);\n  const firstDot = payload.indexOf(SEPARATOR);\n  if (firstDot === -1) return null;\n\n  const secondDot = payload.indexOf(SEPARATOR, firstDot + 1);\n  if (secondDot === -1) return null;\n\n  const gameId = payload.slice(0, firstDot);\n  const versionStr = payload.slice(firstDot + 1, secondDot);\n  const b64 = payload.slice(secondDot + 1);\n\n  if (!gameId || !versionStr || !b64) return null;\n\n  const version = parseInt(versionStr, 10);\n  if (isNaN(version)) return null;\n\n  try {\n    const bytes = fromBase64Url(b64);\n    const json = inflateRaw(bytes, { to: 'string' });\n    const state = JSON.parse(json);\n    return { gameId, version, state };\n  } catch {\n    return null;\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Check whether a URL hash looks like a state URL.\n *\n * @param {string} hash\n * @returns {boolean}\n */\nexport function isStateHash(hash) {\n  if (!hash || typeof hash !== 'string') return false;\n  const raw = hash.startsWith('#') ? hash.slice(1) : hash;\n  return raw.startsWith(HASH_PREFIX);\n}\n\n/**\n * Get encoded URL length for a given state (useful for diagnostics).\n *\n * @param {string} gameId\n * @param {*} state\n * @returns {number}\n */\nexport function encodedLength(gameId, state) {\n  return encodeState(gameId, state).length;\n}\n","numLines":176,"startLine":1,"totalLines":176}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"b86baf8a-e66e-4c72-80fe-55be82785354","session_id":"e2eacce8-17c6-4c8f-9076-7050f49e987b","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/coding/mobile-gaming","sessionId":"e2eacce8-17c6-4c8f-9076-7050f49e987b","version":"2.1.217","gitBranch":"main"}

No DOM or rendering imports.\n *\n * Vehicles slide along their fixed axis (horizontal vehicles move left/right,\n * vertical vehicles move up/down). The hero vehicle must reach the exit.\n *\n * Grid origin: top-left (0, 0). x increases right, y increases down.\n */\n\n/**\n * Create initial game state from level data.\n *\n * @param {Object} level - Level definition\n * @returns {Object} Game state\n */\nexport function createInitialState(level) {\n  const { grid } = level;\n  return {\n    grid: {\n      width: grid.width,\n      height: grid.height,\n      exit: { ...grid.exit }\n    },\n    vehicles: grid.vehicles.map(v => ({ ...v })),\n    moves: 0,\n    status: 'playing'\n  };\n}\n\n/**\n * Build a 2D occupied map: occ[y][x] = vehicleId or null.\n *\n * @param {Object} state\n * @returns {Array[][]} 2D array\n */\nexport function buildOccupied(state) {\n  const { width, height } = state.grid;\n  const occ = Array.from({ length: height }, () => Array(width).fill(null));\n  for (const v of state.vehicles) {\n    if (v.orientation === 'horizontal') {\n      for (let dx = 0; * @returns {boolean}\n */\nexport function hasFileShareSupport() {\n  if (!hasWebShareSupport()) return false;\n  if (typeof navigator.canShare !== 'function') return false;\n  return navigator.canShare({\n    files: [new File([new Blob(['test'], { type: 'text/plain' })], 'test.txt', { type: 'text/plain' })]\n  });\n}\n\n/**\n * Check if running on mobile device\n * @returns || 'Check out my gameplay!',\n      url: url || window.location.href\n    };\n\n    // Add video file if supported\n    if (videoBlob && hasFileShareSupport()) {\n      const fileName = };\n  }\n\n  const { title, text, url } = options;\n  const encodedUrl = encodeURIComponent(url || window.location.href);\n  const encodedText = encodeURIComponent(text || 'Check out my * @param {HTMLCanvasElement} canvas - Game canvas\n * @param {Object} options - Game options\n * @returns {Object} Game controller\n */\nexport function createGame(canvas, options = {}) {\n  const { onWin, onLose, onPinRemoved } = options;\n\n  // Load initial level\n  let gameState = null;\n  let renderer = null;\n  let inputHandler = null;\n  let physicsInterval = null;\n  let hintSession = null;\n\n  /**\n   * Initialize renderer with state\n   * Called once when game is first created\n   */\n  function initRenderer(initialState) {\n    renderer = createRenderer(canvas);\n    renderer.setReducedMotion(isReducedMotionEnabled());\n    renderer.setColorBlindMode(isColorBlindEnabled());\n\n    // Set up input callback\n    if (inputHandler) {\n      inputHandler.destroy();\n    }\n\n    inputHandler = createInput({\n      canvas,\n      renderer,\n      onPinTap: handlePinTap\n    });\n    inputHandler.init();\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Load a level\n   */\n  function renderer.resetAnimations();\n\n    // Reset hint session for new level\n    if (hintSession) { hintSession.destroy(); }\n    hintSession = createHintSession({\n      gameId: 'pull-the-pin',\n      level,\n      getState: () => gameState,\n      onHighlight: ({ move }) => {\n        renderer.setHintPin(move.pinId);\n        render();\n      },\n      onShowMove: ({ move }) => {\n        renderer.setHintPin(move.pinId);\n        render();\n      },\n      onAutoPlay: ({ move }) => {\n        renderer.setHintPin(null);\n        if (gameState && gameState.status === 'playing') {\n          removePin(move.pinId);\n        }\n      },\n      onTokensEmpty: updateHintButton,\n    });\n    updateHintButton();\n\n    // Initial render\n    render();\n\n    return gameState;\n  }\n\n  function updateHintButton() {\n    const btn = document.getElementById('btn-hint');\n    if (!btn) return;\n    const tokens = getHintTokens();\n    btn.textContent = `Hint (${tokens})`;\n    btn.disabled = tokens <= 0;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Handle tap on canvas (called from Phaser scene)\n   */\n  function handlePinTap(pinId) {\n    if (!gameState || gameState.status !== 'playing') return;\n    removePin(pinId);\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Remove a pin and start physics simulation\n   */\n  function removePin(pinId) {\n    renderer.setHintPin(null);\n    gameState = state.removePin(gameState, pinId);\n\n    if (onPinRemoved) {\n      onPinRemoved(pinId);\n    }\n\n    haptic('pin_pull');\n\n    // Pin-pull SFX (gated by the shared soundEnabled setting)\n    resumeAudio();\n    playSound('slide');\n\n    // Start physics simulation\n    startPhysics();\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Start physics simulation loop\n   */\n  function startPhysics() {\n    if (physicsInterval) {\n      clearInterval(physicsInterval);\n    }\n\n    physicsInterval = setInterval(() => {\n      if (!gameState) {\n        stopPhysics();\n        return;\n      }\n\n      // Run one physics step\n      gameState = state.simulateStep(gameState);\n\n      // Check if simulation is done\n      const allDone = gameState.balls.every(b => b.settled || b.lost);\n      if (allDone) {\n        stopPhysics();\n        gameState = { ...gameState, status: state.checkWin(gameState) };\n\n        if (gameState.status === 'won' && onWin) {\n          onWin();\n        } else if (gameState.status === 'lost' && onLose) {\n          onLose();\n        }\n      }\n\n      render();\n      // After physics stops on win, drive confetti animation via loop\n      if (allDone && gameState.status === 'won') renderer.startLoop();\n    }, PHYSICS_TICK_MS);\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Stop physics simulation\n   */\n  function stopPhysics() {\n    if (physicsInterval) {\n
